··················································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································x···x·x······················································································································································································· ······································································································································x····································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································x···························································xx············x··········································································································································································································································································x····x·x···················································································································x·································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································································stdout
